Now L-BFGS high β. We need efficient `lse_softmax_obj_grad`.
Pseudo:
```
def lse_softmax_obj_grad(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x)
    # avoid extreme
    x_clip = np.clip(x, -50, 50)
    p = softmax(x_clip)
    n = len(p)
    c = convolution_fft(p)
    cmax = c.max()
    if beta <= 0: use max weights uniform around max? else
    scaled = beta*(c - cmax)
    # scaled <=0; to avoid underflow? OK
    exp_s = np.exp(scaled)
    ssum = exp_s.sum()
    if ssum <=0: p_w = np.zeros; p_w[argmax]=1
    else p_w = exp_s/ssum
    L = cmax + np.log(ssum)/beta
    # p_w length 2n-1
    rev = p[::-1]
    M2=...; R=fft(rev); Pw=fft(p_w); r=irfft(R*Pw, M2)[n-1:2n-1]
    dL_dp = 2*r  # because derivative wrt p? For S=1
    score = 2*n*L
    grad_p = 2*n*(dL_dp - 2*L)  # since S=1: d(score)/dp =2n (dL_dp -2L)
    # Check: score=2n L; dL/dp; yes. No subtract p*L? Because p parameters with sum constraint not independent. If we use x, need subtract.
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_x = p*(grad_p - mean_grad)
    return score, grad_x
```
But if using softmax, the feasible set is p sum1, no separate scaling. The derivative of objective wrt x is p_i*(grad_p_i - ∑p_j grad_p_j). Correct. Note grad_p is derivative wrt p without sum constraint; mean subtract enforces.
If beta=0? We can set p_w indicator of max or average top. But use β>0.

For high β, exp underflow; ssum count active. If only one active, p_w ~one-hot. L=cmax. Gradient is subgradient averaged over ties. If multiple ties with large β, p_w weights. Good.

`fft_flatten(a, n_iters=200, start_margin=0.5, end_margin=0.05, best=None, time_budget=None)`:

Implementation:
```
def fft_flatten(a, n_iters=200, margin_start=0.6, margin_end=0.0, max_factor=2.0, time_deadline=None, best_sc=None):
    n=len(a); a=a.astype(float64).copy(); S=a.sum(); if S<... return a
    a = a/S
    c=convolution_fft(a); A=np.fft.rfft(a, F) # reuse? We recompute each iter.
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score a using c
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if time_deadline and time.monotonic()>time_deadline: break
       M=c.max(); mean=c.mean(); gap=M-mean
       if gap<1e-15: break
       progress=it/max(1,n_iters-1)
       margin = margin_start + (margin_end-margin_start)*progress  # maybe decreasing
       target = mean + margin*gap
       delta = target - c
       # maybe scale delta? If target below many, large. Could normalize delta by gap.
       delta_spec=np.fft.rfft(delta,F)
       absA2=(A.real*A.real+A.imag*A.imag)
       lam=1e-10*(absA2.max()+1.0)
       D=delta_spec * (A.conj()/(2.0*(absA2+lam)))
       d=np.fft.irfft(D,F)[:n]
       # Normalize step
       # d may have mean not zero; no matter. Compute rough step to keep changes small:
       scale = 0.5
       # relative step: target change in c is delta; but linear solution gives exact first-order. To avoid clipping, limit a changes: max_change = 0.1 * max(a)?
       max_abs_d=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if max_abs_d>0:
           # choose step so that max a*step*|d|? Actually absolute change <= 0.1 * max positive maybe
           pos_min = np.min(a[a>1e-12]) if any positive else 0
           # conservative: step = min(1.0, 0.1 * max(mean_a, 1e-6) / max_abs_d)
          step = min(1.0, max_factor*0.05* max(1.0, a.mean()) / max_abs_d)
       else step=0
       # Try a few steps choose best score if time?
       candidate = a + step*d
       candidate = np.clip(candidate,0,1000)
       S2=candidate.sum(); if S2<... break
       candidate/=S2
       # compute score; if worse significantly, try smaller step
       c2=convolution_fft(candidate); sc2=2*n*c2.max(); # S=1
       if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-15 or it<3:
           a=candidate; c=c2; A=...; update best
       else:
           # try step*0.5
           step*=0.5
           candidate=...
           ... if better update else a unchanged but c same
```
Computing FFT for each try. We can limit one try: If sc2 worse, try half once. Accept if half better than current but maybe not best. Use `current_sc` from c. We need `current_sc` not best. We'll maintain.

```

```
```

Now local high-β LBFGS.

`softmax(x)` top-level:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    m=np.max(x)
    e=np.exp(x-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s==0: return np.ones_like(x)/len(x)
    return e/s
```

def obj_global(x):
    deadline=_STATE.get('deadline', None) if isinstance(_STATE, dict) else None

Now `run_lbfgs_softmax`:
```
def run_lbfgs_softmax(a, beta, maxfun=2000, maxiter=1000, deadline=None, verbose=False):
    n=len(a)
    p=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=p.sum(); if S<... return 1e100,p
    p=p/S
    x0=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-30)); x0-=np.mean(x0)
    x0=np.clip(x0,-30,30)
    bounds=[(-30.0,30.0)]*n
    _STATE['beta']=float(beta); _STATE['deadline']=deadline
    # if n large, maybe maxfun adjusted
    res=minimize(obj_global, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                 options={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':10,'maxcor':20})
    x=res.x
    p=softmax(x); S=p.sum(); p=p/S
    sc=exact_score(p)
    return sc,p
```

But KKT suggests optimal a must be such that convolution with some active y is constant. We can use this as a directional update: Given active weights y, find a' that best satisfies (a*y)_i constant while keeping sum, maybe via log-domain fixed point: a_i ∝ 1/((y*a)_i)? Like mirror descent to flatten convolution with y. Specifically we want (a*y)_i ≈ c. This is nonlinear. Update a_i *= c/(a*y)_i (multiplicative) converges? This resembles solving linear equations. We can alternate: given a, y = top active from h; update a to flatten a*y. This might be powerful.

Define operator T_y(a)_i = ∑_k y_k a_{k-i} = (y_rev? * a) at positions. We want T_y(a) ≈ 1 (or constant). If y supported where h high, decreasing a where high product. This is like solving linear system A_y a ≈ 1 with a≥0. Multiplicative update: a = a * (1 / (A_y a)) (or exp(step(log1 - log(A_ya))). This ensures nonnegative. It's the Euclidean algorithm for linear equations. Could flatten the dual condition.

Algorithm:
- Normalize a sum1.
- For iter: compute h=a*a, M=max, choose active weights y_k = exp(β(y?)(h-M))? Since KKT y supported where h=M. Use y_k normalized sum=1, only top tails.
- Compute z_i = ∑_k y_k a_{k-i} = (rev? convolution) slice. Need z length n. For i: ∑_{k} y_k a_{k-i}. This is same r with p_w=y, a. (r = IFFT(rev_a * y) slice). Note z_i = (a*y)_i? It is nonnegative.
- Update a_i = exp(log a_i + step*(log c - log z_i)), choose c = mean(z) maybe. This equalizes z. Then normalize.
This is an active-set mirror descent directly enforcing KKT for current active set. Could be very effective and cheap (2 FFT per iter). We can combine with h-based adjustments.

Let's test intuition: If y is uniform over all active lags where h=M, z_i is weighted sum of a's that contribute to peaks. Reducing a_i with large z_i reduces peaks. This is similar to gradient but multiplicative, stable. It may drive towards a positive solution where z constant. If active set changes, iterate.

Implement `dual_flatten(a, iters, beta_active, step)`:
```
a normalized
for it:
 h=conv(a); M=max; gap=M-mean
 y=softmax(beta*(h-M)) (length 2n-1) # active high
 # Maybe zero out below M - tol if beta high enough
 rev_a=a[::-1]
 c=conv(rev_a,y) slice n = z
 # c_i has dimension; normalize z to mean1
 c_mean=z.mean()
 # multiplicative update: a *= (c_mean/z)^eta, with floor
 ratio = np.exp(np.clip(eta*(np.log(c_mean) - np.log(z+eps)), -3,3))
 a_new = a * ratio
 a_new=clip; normalize
 if score improved adopt
```
This doesn't directly reduce h but enforces KKT. Could produce good trials. We can also use `z = c + eps` to avoid huge ratios. eta schedule 0.1-0.5.

Not too high to avoid zero. Good.

```

def fft_flatten(a, n_iters=200, deadline=None, margin_start=0.7, margin_end=0.15, max_step=0.5, label=''):
    n=len(a)

def mirror_active(a, n_iters=200, deadline=None, beta_factor=20.0, step=0.3):
 n; normalize; F; A,c=…

def proposal_to_list(a):
   n=len(a); target=math.sqrt(2.0*n)
   out=np.asarray(a,float)*target
   out=np.clip(out,0,1000)
   S=out.sum()
   if S<0.01: out=target*np.ones(n)
   return [float(x) for x in out]
```

Now, let's double-check `lse_softmax_obj_grad` for performance and correctness.
```
def lse_softmax_obj_grad(x, beta):
    n=int(len(x))
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    # clip x to avoid exp issues
    x=np.clip(x,-50.0,50.0)
    m=np.max(x)
    e=np.exp(x-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    p=e/s
    c=convolution_fft(p) # length 2n-1, sum1, max
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0 or beta < 1e-12:
       # use active top? set p_w uniform over entries with c>=cmax-1e-12? 
       idx=np.where(c >= cmax - 1e-12)[0]
       p_w=np.zeros(2*n-1); p_w[idx]=1.0/len(idx)
       L=cmax
    else:
       scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
       # scaled max 0; underflow ok
       exp_s=np.exp(scaled)
       exp_s_sum=float(np.sum(exp_s))
       if exp_s_sum <=0 or not finite:
          p_w=zeros; p_w[argmax]=1; L=cmax
       else:
          p_w=exp_s/exp_s_sum
          L=cmax + math.log(exp_s_sum)/float(beta)
    # if L not finite return
    rev=p[::-1]
    M2=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
    R=np.fft.rfft(rev, M2)
    W=np.fft.rfft(p_w, M2)
    r=np.fft.irfft(R*W, M2)
    r=r[n-1:2*n-1]
    # use L in gradient? For score 2nL, unnormalized grad =4n(r - L? wait L dimension)
    grad_p = 4.0*float(n)*(r - L)  # L is scalar
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_x = p*(grad_p - mean_grad)
    score=2.0*float(n)*L
    # clip gradient? if not finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)): grad_x=np.zeros(n)
    return float(score), grad_x
```
Check `r - L`: r has dimension? r_i =∑ p_w a_j. If p_w sum1, r mean ~mean a? L~cmax. For uniform, p=1/n, p_w uniform over 2n-1, c=triangular, L ~? r_i for central active? Could be ~1/n? L~1/n. r-L could be negative/positive. OK.

But is L in gradient should be score/2n? L is LSE of c, not L/S² because S=1. Yes.

Wait `grad_p = 4n (r - L)` or `4n (r - L*1)`? The unnormalized gradient formula: 2n(dL/da/S² -2L/S³ dS/da). dL/da=2r. S=1. dS/da=1. So 2n(2r -2L) =4n(r - L). Correct.

Then `mean_grad = p·grad_p`. Is projection `p*(grad_p - mean_grad)` correct for softmax? ∂p_j/∂x_i = p_j(δ_{ij}-p_i). So ∂F/∂x_i =∑_j grad_p_j p_j(δ_{ij}-p_i)=p_i grad_p_i - p_i∑p_j grad_p_j = p_i(grad_p_i - mean). Yes.

If beta LSE > exact, objective value not exact; for scoring candidates use exact. In L-BFGS, if LSE score improves but exact worse, we track exact after run. OK.

Potential issue: `grad_x` scale may be too large for high n. We can optionally scale gradient by `1.0` no.

Let's test L-BFGS with beta high: p_w one-hot, L=cmax. r_i = a_{k-i}. grad_p=4n(a_{k-i}-L). This may have large positive where a low? The projection subtract mean. OK.

Now `convolution_fft(p)` for p normalized but p from softmax sum may have tiny errors; c sum ~1. Use `p = p / p.sum()` after softmax and before conv.

def build_initializations(n,parent,rng,max_inits=6):
 inits=[]
 if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
   if len(parent)==n: p=parent.copy()
   else: p=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,len(parent)), parent)
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   if p.sum()>0: inits.append(p)
 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 inits.append(np.ones(n)*0.5)
 # shapes
 for arr in ...:
   arr=np.clip(arr,1e-3,1000)
   if len(arr)==n and np.isfinite(arr).all(): inits.append(arr)
 # random
 inits.append(np.clip(0.5+0.2*rng.standard_normal(n),1e-3,None))
 return inits[:max_inits]
```

Let's write `fft_flatten` carefully:
```
def fft_flatten(a, n_iters=200, deadline=None, margin_start=0.7, margin_end=0.1, base_scale=0.35):
    n=len(a); a=a.astype(...); S=a.sum(); if S<...: return normalized ones
    a=a/S
    F=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,F); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,F); ml=2*n-1; c=c[:ml]
    cmax=float(np.max(c)); current_sc=2*n*cmax
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=current_sc
    no_progress=0
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       M=float(c.max()); mean=float(c.mean()); gap=M-mean
       if gap < 1e-16: break
       prog=it/float(max(1,n_iters-1))
       margin=margin_start + (margin_end-margin_start)*prog
       target=mean + margin*gap
       err=np.maximum(c-target,0.0)
       if err.max() < 1e-18:
          # if all at target, reduce target in two stages? break or use c-M? 
          if margin > 0.5*margin_end:
             target = mean + 0.99*margin*gap
          else: break
       err_spec=np.fft.rfft(err,F)
       absA2=A.real*A.real + A.imag*A.imag
       lam=1e-12*(float(np.max(absA2))+1.0)
       denom=2.0*(absA2+lam)
       D=-err_spec * (A.conj()/denom)
       d=np.fft.irfft(D,F)[:n]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)): break
       mad=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if mad <= 1e-30: break
       mean_a=float(a.mean())
       damp=0.25+0.55*prog
       base_step=damp*base_scale
       if mad > 1e-12:
          base_step=min(base_step, 0.25*mean_a/mad)
       # ensure not zero
       steps=[]
       s=base_step
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
          vals=[s*mult] if not in steps
       improved=False
       for st in steps:
          cand,A2,c2,sc = try_update(a,d,st,F,n)
          if cand is None: continue
          if sc < current_sc - 1e-15:
             a=cand; A=A2; c=c2[:ml]; current_sc=sc; improved=True
             if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
             no_progress=0
             break
       if not improved:
          no_progress +=1
          if no_progress > 30: break
    return best_a
```
But if step list includes very small, may accept tiny improvements; ok. However if best_sc for parent is slightly better than current after no improve, we return best snapshot; current may stagnant. Good.

`try_update` top-level:
```
def try_update_current(a,d,step,F,n):
    cand=a + step*d
    np.clip(cand,0.0,1000.0,out=cand)
    S=float(cand.sum())
    if S<1e-12 or not finite: return None,None,None,None
    cand = cand/S
    A=np.fft.rfft(cand,F)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,F)[:2*n-1]
    sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
    if not finite: return None...
    return cand,A,c,sc
```

```
def mirror_active(a,n_iters=150,deadline=None,beta_factor=20.0,base_step=0.3):
 normalize; F,M2; A,c; best; no_progress=0
 for it:
   if time>deadline break
   M=max; mean=mean; gap=M-mean; if gap<1e-15: break
   prog=...
   beta=beta_factor/max(gap,1e-15)
   scaled=beta*(c-M)
   y=np.exp(scaled); sy=float(np.sum(y))
   if sy<=0 or not finite: y=zeros; y[argmax]=1 else y/=sy
   rev=a[::-1]
   R=np.fft.rfft(rev,M2); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,M2); z=np.fft.irfft(R*Y,M2)
   z=z[n-1:2*n-1]
   z=np.maximum(z,1e-300)
   target=float(np.mean(z))
   if target<=0: break
   step=base_step*(0.5+0.5*prog)
   for st in (step,0.5*step,0.25*step):
      log_ratio=st*(np.log(target)-np.log(z))
      log_ratio=np.clip(log_ratio,-1.5,1.5)
      cand=a*np.exp(log_ratio)
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); S=cand.sum(); if S<... continue
      cand/=S
      A2,c2 = conv; sc=...
      if sc<current_sc: adopt; break
   if no improved no_progress++
   if no_progress>30 break
 return best_a
```
Note M2 computed once. If z has zeros (possible), log negative large; clip.

def polish_sequence(a, deadline, phases=4):
    n=len(a)
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    # split list of fractions [0.15 flatten,0.15 mirror,0.45 lbfgs,0.15 final]

    a=fft_flatten(a, n_iters=400, deadline=d1)
    d2=t0+0.30*remaining
    a=mirror_active(a, n_iters=200, deadline=d2)
    # adapt best
    a=choose_best([a,...])
    # lbfgs for remaining until deadline-30
    multipliers=[1,2,5,15,40]
    # allocate allowed per mult decreasing
    for idx,mult in enumerate(multipliers):
       if time > deadline-25: break
       allowed = max(10, (deadline - time.monotonic())/len(multipliers[1:])? ) # simplistic
       if idx==0: allowed=0.3*(deadline-now)
       elif idx==1: 0.25...
       elif idx==2: 0.2...

       elif idx==4:0.1...
       gap=...; beta=mult/gap
       sc,p=run_lbfgs_softmax(a,beta,allowed_s=allowed,deadline=deadline)
       if improve: a=p; best
       # quick flatten if time
    # final flatten and mirror small
    return best

`try_flatten_step` top-level:
```
def try_flatten_step(a,d,step,F,n):
   cand = a + step*d
   np.clip(cand,0.0,1000.0,out=cand) # cand writeable
   S=float(cand.sum())
   if S<=1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return None,None,None,None
   cand = cand / S
   A = np.fft.rfft(cand,F)
   c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,F)
   c = c[:2*n-1]
   sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(sc): return None,None,None,None
   return cand,A,c,sc
```

Implement `mirror_active`:
```
def mirror_active(a,n_iters=200,deadline=None,beta_factor=20.0,base_step=0.3):
 n; normalize; F; A,c; current_sc; best; no_progress=0; M2=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
 for it:
   if deadline and time>deadline break
   M=max(c); mean=mean; gap=M-mean
   if gap<1e-16: break
   prog=...
   beta=max(1e-12, beta_factor/max(gap,1e-300))
   scaled=beta*(c-M)
   y=np.exp(scaled)
   sy=float(np.sum(y))
   if sy<=...: y=np.zeros; y[argmax]=1 else y/=sy
   rev=a[::-1]
   R=np.fft.rfft(rev,M2); Y=np.fft.rfft(y,M2)
   z=np.fft.irfft(R*Y,M2)[n-1:2*n-1]
   z=np.maximum(z,1e-300)
   target=max(float(np.mean(z)),1e-300)
   steps=[]; step=base_step*(0.3+0.7*prog); for mult...
   improved=False
   for st in steps:
      log_ratio=st*(np.log(target)-np.log(z))
      np.clip(log_ratio,-2.0,2.0,out=log_ratio)
      cand=a*np.exp(log_ratio)
      if not finite: continue
      S=cand.sum(); if S<...: continue
      cand=cand/S
      A2=np.fft.rfft(cand,F)
      c2=np.fft.irfft(A2*A2,F)[:ml]
      sc2=2*n*float(np.max(c2))
      if not finite: continue
      if sc2 < current_sc - 1e-15:
         a=cand; A=A2; c=c2; current_sc=sc2; improved=True
         if sc2<best_sc: best_a=...; best_sc=sc2
         break
   if not improved: no_progress++ else 0
   if no_progress>40: break
 return best_a
```
This only accepts improving steps. If direction slightly bad but could be good after, no progress. Could alternate with fft_flatten.

Now `polish_sequence` implement:
```
def polish_sequence(a, deadline, name='')
    # initial flatten
    tstart=time.monotonic()
    total=max(10, deadline-tstart)
    d= tstart + 0.18*total
    a1=fft_flatten(a, n_iters=500, deadline=d)
    d2=tstart+0.33*total
    a2=mirror_active(a1, n_iters=300, deadline=d2)
    # choose best
    sc1=exact_score(a1); sc2=exact_score(a2); also original
    a = best among
    # L-BFGS multipliers

`benchmark_lbfgs_eval`:
```
def benchmark_lbfgs_eval(x,beta,count=2):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(count):
       lse_softmax_obj_grad(x,beta)
    return (time.monotonic()-t0)/float(max(1,count))
```

`softmax` top-level. `log_probs_from_a` top-level:
```
def log_probs_from_a(a):
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum(); if S<...: return zeros
    p=a/S
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
    x=np.log(p)
    x-=float(np.mean(x))
    return np.clip(x,-30,30)
```

`build_initializations` top-level. Need pass `max_inits`; should ensure arrays not too many.
```
def build_initializations(n,parent,rng,max_inits=6):
 inits=[]
 if parent is not None ... append
 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 arrs=[]
 arrs.append(np.ones(n))
 arrs.append(0.1 + 0.9*x)
 arrs.append(0.1 + 0.9*(1.0-x))
 for q in (0.3,0.7,1.5,2.5):
    arrs.append(np.maximum(x**q,1e-3))
    arrs.append(np.maximum((1.0-x)**q,1e-3))
 for freq in (1,2,3):
    arrs.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x))
    arrs.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x))
 # sin^2
 arrs.append(0.2 + 0.8*np.sin(np.pi*x)**2)
 # two gaussians
 width=0.15
 arrs.append(0.5*np.exp(-((x-0.25)/width)**2)+0.5*np.exp(-((x-0.75)/width)**2)+1e-3)
 # alternating
 i=np.arange(n)
 arrs.append((0.3+0.7*x)*(1.0+0.2*np.cos(np.pi*i)))
 # random smooth? rng.standard_normal
 arrs.append(np.clip(0.5+0.2*rng.standard_normal(n),1e-3,None))
 for arr in arrs:
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
   if len(arr)!=n or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): continue
   arr=np.clip(arr,1e-4,1000)
   if arr.sum()>0:
      inits.append(arr/arr.sum())
   if len(inits)>=max_inits: break
 return inits
```
If max_inits small but parent should first. OK.

Define `resample_array`:
```
def resample_array(arr,n):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
   m=len(arr)
   if m==0: return np.ones(n)
   if m==n: return arr.copy()
   xold=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return np.interp(xnew,xold,arr)
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2)
over non-negative sequences a of length n.

Algorithm:
- Normalize each candidate to sum 1 (objective is scale invariant).
- Use several robust initializations (parent interpolation, ramps,
  geometric tapers, sinusoids, experts).
- Cheap FFT-based projection/flattening that reduces peaks of A(z)^2.
- Mirror-descent-like dual active-set polishing based on active conv peaks.
- High-temperature LSE smoothing of the max object, optimized in log-softmax
  variables with L-BFGS-B.
- Multi-length exploration, perturbation restarts, and final scaling.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_STATE = {"beta": 1.0, "deadline": None}


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def convolution_fft(a, F=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if F is None:
        F = next_power_of_two(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)
    return b[:size]


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.ones_like(x) / max(1, len(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(x) / max(1, len(x))
    return e / s


def log_probs_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(len(a))
    p = a / S
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    x = np.log(p)
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    return np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0)


def lse_softmax_obj_grad(x, beta):
    """
    LSE-smoothed score and gradient with respect to log-softmax variables x.
    The underlying sequence is p = softmax(x), sum(p)=1.
    Objective: 2n * LSE_beta(conv(p,p)).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)

    beta = float(beta)
    p = softmax(x)

    c = convolution_fft(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)

    if beta <= 1e-12:
        idx = np.where(c >= cmax - 1e-14)[0]
        if len(idx) == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
        p_w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p_w[idx] = 1.0 / float(len(idx))
        L = cmax
    else:
        scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
        exp_s = np.exp(scaled)
        s = float(np.sum(exp_s))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            p_w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            p_w[idx] = 1.0
            L = cmax
        else:
            p_w = exp_s / s
            L = cmax + math.log(s) / beta

    rev_p = p[::-1]
    M2 = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    R = np.fft.rfft(rev_p, M2)
    W = np.fft.rfft(p_w, M2)
    r = np.fft.irfft(R * W, M2)
    r = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    # Unconstrained gradient of the scale-invariant score at sum(p)=1:
    # d(score)/dp = 4*n*(r - L).  Then project to simplex tangent for softmax.
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * (r - L)
    mean_grad = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    grad_x = p * (grad_p - mean_grad)

    score = 2.0 * float(n) * L
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        grad_x = np.zeros(n)
    return float(score), grad_x


def obj_global(x):
    dl = _STATE.get("deadline", None)
    if dl is not None and time.monotonic() > float(dl):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x), dtype=np.float64)
    beta = _STATE.get("beta", 1.0)
    return lse_softmax_obj_grad(x, beta)


def benchmark_lbfgs_eval(x, beta, count=2):
    cnt = max(1, int(count))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(cnt):
        lse_softmax_obj_grad(x, beta)
    return (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(cnt)


def run_lbfgs_softmax(a, beta, allowed_s, deadline, maxfun_cap=3000):
    n = len(np.asarray(a))
    if deadline is None or (time.monotonic() + 5.0) > float(deadline):
        sc = exact_score(a)
        return sc, a

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n) / n
    else:
        a = a / S

    x0 = log_probs_from_a(a)
    beta = float(beta)
    allowed_s = max(1.0, float(allowed_s))
    stop_deadline = min(float(deadline), time.monotonic() + 1.25 * allowed_s)
    per_eval = benchmark_lbfgs_eval(x0, beta, count=2)
    if per_eval <= 1e-6 or not np.isfinite(per_eval):
        per_eval = max(1e-5, (1.0 / max(1.0, n)) * 0.02)

    maxfun = int(max(20, min(maxfun_cap, int((allowed_s * 0.80) / max(per_eval, 1e-6)))))
    maxiter = int(max(20, maxfun))

    _STATE["beta"] = beta
    _STATE["deadline"] = stop_deadline

    bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj_global,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "maxls": 10,
                "maxcor": 20,
            },
        )
        x1 = res.x
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x1)):
            x1 = x0
    except Exception:
        x1 = x0

    p = softmax(x1)
    p = p / max(float(np.sum(p)), 1e-300)
    sc = exact_score(p)
    return float(sc), p


def try_flatten_step(a, d, step, F, n):
    cand = a + step * d
    np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0, out=cand)
    S = float(np.sum(cand))
    if S < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return None, None, None, None
    cand = cand / S
    A = np.fft.rfft(cand, F)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)[: 2 * n - 1]
    sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, None, None, None
    return cand, A, c, float(sc)


def fft_flatten(a, n_iters=300, deadline=None, margin_start=0.75, margin_end=0.10, base_scale=0.35):
    """
    First-order FFT flattening of A(z)^2 coefficients.
    Works on a normalized (sum=1) sequence and returns best found (a, score).
    """
    n = len(a)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n) / max(1, n)
    else:
        a = a / S

    F = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)[: 2 * n - 1]
    current_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc
    no_progress = 0

    for it in range(max(0, int(n_iters))):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        M = float(np.max(c))
        mean = float(np.mean(c))
        gap = M - mean
        if gap < 1e-16:
            break

        prog = float(it) / float(max(1, int(n_iters) - 1))
        margin = margin_start + (margin_end - margin_start) * prog
        target = mean + margin * gap
        err = np.maximum(c - target, 0.0)
        if float(np.max(err)) < 1e-18:
            no_progress += 1
            if no_progress > 30:
                break
            continue

        err_spec = np.fft.rfft(err, F)
        absA2 = A.real * A.real + A.imag * A.imag
        lam = 1e-12 * (float(np.max(absA2)) + 1.0)
        denom = 2.0 * (absA2 + lam)
        D = -err_spec * (A.conj() / denom)
        d = np.fft.irfft(D, F)[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            break

        mad = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if mad <= 1e-30:
            break

        mean_a = float(np.mean(a))
        damp = 0.25 + 0.55 * prog
        base_step = damp * base_scale
        if mad > 1e-12:
            base_step = min(base_step, 0.25 * mean_a / mad)
        base_step = min(1.0, max(1e-4, base_step))

        improved = False
        steps = (base_step, 0.5 * base_step, 0.25 * base_step, 0.1 * base_step)
        for st in steps:
            cand, A2, c2, sc2 = try_flatten_step(a, d, st, F, n)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            if sc2 < current_sc - 1e-15:
                a = cand
                A = A2
                c = c2
                current_sc = sc2
                improved = True
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                no_progress = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            no_progress += 1
            if no_progress > 60:
                break

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def mirror_active(a, n_iters=200, deadline=None, beta_factor=20.0, base_step=0.30):
    """
    Active-set mirror update:
      - y_k is concentrated on currently large coefficients c_k,
      - z_i = sum_k y_k a_{k-i},
      - multiplicatively adjust a_i to make z_i flatter.
    This enforces a KKT-like dual condition on the active peaks.
    """
    n = len(a)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n) / max(1, n)
    else:
        a = a / S

    F = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    M2 = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, F)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, F)[: 2 * n - 1]
    current_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc
    no_progress = 0

    for it in range(max(0, int(n_iters))):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        M = float(np.max(c))
        mean = float(np.mean(c))
        gap = M - mean
        if gap < 1e-16:
            break

        prog = float(it) / float(max(1, int(n_iters) - 1))
        beta = float(beta_factor) / max(gap, 1e-300)
        scaled = beta * (c - M)
        y = np.exp(scaled)
        sy = float(np.sum(y))
        if sy <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sy):
            y = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            y[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            y = y / sy

        rev_a = a[::-1]
        R = np.fft.rfft(rev_a, M2)
        Y = np.fft.rfft(y, M2)
        z = np.fft.irfft(R * Y, M2)
        z = z[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        z = np.maximum(z, 1e-300)

        target = max(float(np.mean(z)), 1e-300)
        if not np.isfinite(target):
            break

        step = base_step * (0.35 + 0.65 * prog)
        if step <= 1e-12:
            break

        improved = False
        steps = (step, 0.5 * step, 0.25 * step)
        for st in steps:
            log_ratio = st * (np.log(target) - np.log(z))
            np.clip(log_ratio, -1.5, 1.5, out=log_ratio)
            cand = a * np.exp(log_ratio)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                continue
            S2 = float(np.sum(cand))
            if S2 < 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = cand / S2
            A2 = np.fft.rfft(cand, F)
            c2 = np.fft.irfft(A2 * A2, F)[: 2 * n - 1]
            sc2 = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c2))
            if not np.isfinite(sc2):
                continue
            if sc2 < current_sc - 1e-15:
                a = cand
                A = A2
                c = c2
                current_sc = sc2
                improved = True
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                no_progress = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            no_progress += 1
            if no_progress > 60:
                break

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def choose_best_a(a_list):
    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for a in a_list:
        if a is None:
            continue
        try:
            sc = exact_score(a)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(128)
        best_sc = 2.0
    return best, float(best_sc)


def resample_array(arr, n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if len(arr) == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, n))
    if len(arr) == int(n):
        return arr.copy()
    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(arr))
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    return np.interp(xnew, xold, arr)


def build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_inits=6):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)

    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        p = resample_array(parent, n)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(np.sum(p))
        if S > 1e-9 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            inits.append(p / S)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    arrs = []

    # Useful monotone/taper shapes.
    arrs.append(0.1 + 0.9 * x)
    arrs.append(0.1 + 0.9 * (1.0 - x))

    # Geometric tapers (both directions).
    for logq in (-0.20, -0.08, -0.03, 0.03, 0.08, 0.20):
        arrs.append(np.exp(np.clip(logq * i, -50.0, 50.0)))

    # Low-frequency sinusoidal perturbations.
    for freq in (1, 2, 3):
        arrs.append(1.0 + 0.55 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x))
        arrs.append(1.0 + 0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x))

    # Power laws.
    for q in (0.35, 0.7, 1.5, 2.5):
        arrs.append(np.maximum(x ** q, 1e-4))
        arrs.append(np.maximum((1.0 - x) ** q, 1e-4))

    # Zero endpoint shapes.
    arrs.append(0.2 + 0.8 * np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)

    # Two Gaussians.
    w = 0.16
    arrs.append(
        0.5 * np.exp(-((x - 0.25) / w) ** 2)
        + 0.5 * np.exp(-((x - 0.75) / w) ** 2)
        + 1e-4
    )

    # Mild high-frequency alternation times envelope.
    arrs.append((0.3 + 0.7 * x) * (1.0 + 0.15 * np.cos(np.pi * i)))

    # Random smooth-like seed.
    arrs.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.18 * rng.standard_normal(n), 1e-3, 1000.0))

    # Fallback constant last.
    arrs.append(np.ones(n) * 0.5)

    need = max(0, int(max_inits) - len(inits))
    for arr in arrs[:need]:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
            if len(arr) != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                continue
            arr = np.clip(arr, 1e-5, 1000.0)
            S = float(np.sum(arr))
            if S > 1e-9:
                inits.append(arr / S)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return inits


def polish_sequence(a, deadline):
    """
    Polish one sequence inside an absolute deadline.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    tstart = time.monotonic()
    total = max(20.0, float(deadline) - tstart)

    orig = a.copy()
    orig_sc = exact_score(orig)
    best_a, best_sc = orig, float(orig_sc)

    def consider(a_new, sc_new=None):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if a_new is None:
            return
        if sc_new is None:
            sc_new = exact_score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best_a = np.asarray(a_new, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    if deadline is None or (time.monotonic() + 20.0) > float(deadline):
        return best_a, best_sc

    # Stage 1: cheaper global flattening.
    d1 = tstart + 0.18 * total
    a1, sc1 = fft_flatten(a, n_iters=1000, deadline=d1)
    consider(a1, sc1)

    # Stage 2: active mirror uplift.
    d2 = tstart + 0.32 * total
    a2, sc2 = mirror_active(a1, n_iters=800, deadline=d2)
    consider(a2, sc2)

    # Stage 3: LSE + L-BFGS with adaptive beta.
    multipliers = (1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 12.0, 30.0, 60.0)
    if len(multipliers) == 1:
        fracs = (1.0,)
    elif len(multipliers) == 2:
        fracs = (0.65, 0.35)
    elif len(multipliers) == 3:
        fracs = (0.55, 0.30, 0.15)
    else:
        fracs = (0.22, 0.18, 0.15, 0.12, 0.10, 0.08)

    # Reserve a little time for final cheap polishing.
    lbfgs_end = float(deadline) - max(15.0, 0.08 * total)

    for mult, frac in zip(multipliers, fracs):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > lbfgs_end - 12.0:
            break
        allowed = max(8.0, frac * total)
        allowed = min(allowed, lbfgs_end - now - 5.0)
        if allowed < 4.0:
            break

        c = convolution_fft(best_a)
        gap = max(float(np.max(c)) - float(np.mean(c)), 1e-15)
        beta = float(mult) / gap

        p_new, sc_new = run_lbfgs_softmax(
            best_a,
            beta=beta,
            allowed_s=allowed,
            deadline=lbfgs_end,
            maxfun_cap=2800,
        )
        consider(p_new, sc_new)

        # Very short re-flatten after high-beta moves.
        if mult >= 12.0:
            extra_end = min(lbfgs_end, time.monotonic() + max(8.0, 0.5 * allowed))
            p_flat, sc_flat = fft_flatten(p_new, n_iters=200, deadline=extra_end)
            consider(p_flat, sc_flat)

    # Final cheap stage.
    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 8.0:
        dfin = float(deadline) - 5.0
        a_fin1, sc_fin1 = fft_flatten(best_a, n_iters=500, deadline=dfin)
        consider(a_fin1, sc_fin1)
        a_fin2, sc_fin2 = mirror_active(a_fin1, n_iters=300, deadline=dfin)
        consider(a_fin2, sc_fin2)

    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def make_output_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return [0.5]

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))

    if S < 1e-9:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = a / S
        target_sum = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
        out = out * target_sum
        np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0, out=out)

    if float(np.sum(out)) < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
        np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0, out=out)

    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    MAX_N = 16384

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 64 and p.sum() > 0:
            if len(p) > MAX_N:
                p = resample_array(p, MAX_N)
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        base_len = len(parent)
        base_len = int(round(base_len / 16.0) * 16)
        base_len = max(1024, min(MAX_N, base_len))
    else:
        base_len = 4096

    lengths = []

    def add_length(n):
        n = int(round(float(n) / 16.0) * 16)
        if 1024 <= n <= MAX_N:
            if n not in lengths:
                lengths.append(n)

    add_length(base_len)
    add_length(int(base_len * 1.35))
    add_length(int(base_len * 1.70))
    if parent is None:
        add_length(6144)
        add_length(8192)
    add_length(min(MAX_N, int(base_len * 2.35)))

    lengths = sorted(lengths)[:4]
    print(f"[Parent] present={parent is not None}, base_len={base_len}, lengths={lengths}", flush=True)

    phase1_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 0.28 * (budget_s - 20.0))
    per_len_best = {}

    for n in lengths:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > phase1_deadline - 10.0:
            break

        est_inits = max(1, int((phase1_deadline - now) / 30.0) + 1)
        inits = build_initializations(n, parent, rng, max_inits=max(1, est_inits))
        if not inits:
            inits = [np.ones(n) * 0.5]

        local_best = None
        local_sc = np.inf

        remaining_count = len(inits)
        for idx, a0 in enumerate(inits):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > phase1_deadline - 8.0:
                break
            alloc = (phase1_deadline - now) / float(max(1, remaining_count - idx))
            alloc = max(6.0, min(60.0, alloc * 1.05))
            init_deadline = min(phase1_deadline, now + alloc)

            n_flat = int(max(40, min(260, 1700000.0 / max(1, n))))
            n_mirror = int(max(24, n_flat // 3))

            a1, sc1 = fft_flatten(a0, n_iters=n_flat, deadline=init_deadline)
            a2, sc2 = mirror_active(a1, n_iters=n_mirror, deadline=init_deadline)

            local_seq, local_sc = choose_best_a([a0, a1, a2])
            if local_sc < local_sc:
                local_best, local_sc = local_seq, local_sc
                print(f"  phase1 n={n} init={idx}: {local_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

            remaining_count -= 1

        if local_best is not None:
            per_len_best[int(n)] = (float(local_sc), local_best)
            print(f"  phase1 done n={n}: best={local_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    if not per_len_best:
        a0 = np.ones(max(2048, min(MAX_N, base_len)), dtype=np.float64)
        per_len_best[max(2048, min(MAX_N, base_len))] = (2.0, a0)

    order = []
    for n, val in per_len_best.items():
        order.append((val[0], n))
    order.sort()

    chosen = []
    for sc, n in order:
        if len(chosen) >= 2:
            break
        chosen.append(n)

    max_len = max(per_len_best.keys())
    if max_len not in chosen and len(chosen) < 3 and (order[-1][0] - order[0][0]) <= 0.06:
        chosen.append(max_len)

    if not chosen:
        chosen = [max_len]

    best_a, best_sc = None, np.inf
    for sc, n in per_len_best.items():
        a = per_len_best[n][1]
        if best_a is None or sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    print(f"Best before stage2: {best_sc:.8f} (n={0 if best_a is None else len(best_a)})", flush=True)

    stage2_end = min(deadline - 25.0, t0 + 0.82 * (budget_s - 20.0))
    total_stage2 = max(0.0, stage2_end - time.monotonic())
    if total_stage2 > 45.0:
        if len(chosen) == 1:
            fracs = (1.0,)
        elif len(chosen) == 2:
            fracs = (0.68, 0.32)
        else:
            fracs = (0.55, 0.30, 0.15)

        for idx, n in enumerate(chosen):
            if time.monotonic() > stage2_end - 15.0:
                break
            if idx >= len(fracs):
                frac = 0.1
            else:
                frac = fracs[idx]
            alloc = max(20.0, frac * total_stage2)
            alloc = min(alloc, stage2_end - time.monotonic() - 8.0)
            if alloc < 15.0:
                break

            a0 = per_len_best[int(n)][1]
            end_run = time.monotonic() + alloc
            print(f"  stage2 polishing n={n}, allowed~{alloc:.0f}s", flush=True)
            a_pol, sc_pol = polish_sequence(a0, deadline=end_run)
            if sc_pol < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_pol
                best_a = a_pol
                print(f"  NEW BEST {sc_pol:.8f} (n={len(a_pol)})", flush=True)

    # Perturbation restarts from the global best, if time remains.
    if best_a is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 140.0:
        n_best = len(best_a)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_best)
        restart_end = deadline - 30.0
        trial_alloc = (restart_end - time.monotonic()) / 4.0

        for trial in range(4):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > restart_end - 15.0:
                break
            end_try = min(restart_end, now + max(10.0, trial_alloc))

            if trial == 0:
                a_pert = best_a * (1.0 + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n_best))
            elif trial == 1:
                freq = 1.0 + 4.0 * rng.random()
                ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
                a_pert = best_a * (1.0 + 0.12 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph))
            elif trial == 2:
                other = None
                for nn, (sc, aa) in per_len_best.items():
                    if nn != n_best and aa is not None:
                        other = resample_array(aa, n_best)
                        break
                if other is None:
                    other = 0.1 + 0.9 * x
                other = np.clip(other, 0.0, 1000.0)
                Sother = float(np.sum(other))
                if Sother > 0:
                    other = other / Sother
                a_pert = 0.75 * best_a + 0.25 * other
            else:
                # Block rescale.
                a_pert = best_a.copy()
                nb = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
                block = max(1, n_best // nb)
                for b in range(min(nb, n_best // block)):
                    s = b * block
                    e = min(n_best, s + block)
                    if e > s:
                        a_pert[s:e] *= 0.7 + 0.6 * rng.random()

            a_pert = np.clip(a_pert, 0.0, 1000.0)
            a_flat, sc_flat = fft_flatten(a_pert, n_iters=80, deadline=end_try)
            a_mir, sc_mir = mirror_active(a_flat, n_iters=50, deadline=end_try)
            a_c, sc_c = choose_best_a([a_pert, a_flat, a_mir])

            c = convolution_fft(a_c)
            gap = max(float(np.max(c)) - float(np.mean(c)), 1e-15)
            beta = 8.0 / gap
            allowed = max(4.0, (end_try - time.monotonic()) * 0.7)
            a_new, sc_new = run_lbfgs_softmax(
                a_c,
                beta=beta,
                allowed_s=allowed,
                deadline=end_try,
                maxfun_cap=1200,
            )
            if sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_a = a_new
                print(f"  restart {trial}: NEW BEST {sc_new:.8f} (n={len(a_new)})", flush=True)

    # Final safety / fallback.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        fallback_n = min(MAX_N, max(2048, base_len))
        best_a = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_sc = 2.0

    # If best is unexpectedly long, downsample for safety.
    if len(best_a) > MAX_N:
        best_a = resample_array(best_a, MAX_N)
        best_sc = exact_score(best_a)

    out = make_output_from_a(best_a)

    verified = None
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0 and len(out) <= 12000:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = None

    final_sc = verified if verified is not None else best_sc
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.8f}, verified={verified if verified is not None else 'n/a'}", flush=True)
    print(f"OUTPUT length={len(out)}, time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}/{budget_s:.1f}s", flush=True)
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)

    return out
```
